# CLAIMS.md

This is the claims ledger for the t project. It lists every measurable claim found in the t docs and in ROADMAP.md, generated 2026-09-10 by an audit of 1567 claims. Each claim was checked against a witness: a committed table, a run output under t/out/, a job log, a ground-truth or fuzz-family results.json, a banked table copy, a launch script, or a commit message. A claim is WITNESSED when the cited source states the claim as written and was inspected directly. A claim is REPRODUCED when a cheap, bounded re-run this session (a census script, a test file, a round trip, or a grade on an existing sweep directory) recomputed the same number. A claim is STALE when the witness exists but no longer matches: the file it points to has since changed, usually because a later sweep or commit overwrote the table, so the number was true at some point but is not true of the file as committed now. A claim is UNWITNESSED when no committed table, run output, log, or commit could be found that states the number. NOT-A-MEASUREMENT marks a handful of lines that were pulled in during extraction but turn out to be descriptive text rather than a checkable number or fact. Nothing in this ledger was invented: where no witness could be found, the row says so instead of guessing one.

Read the ledger by status first if you want the action list: the "The action list, as worked" section right below collects every claim that was STALE or UNWITNESSED, now shown with the action taken, grouped by file, since those are the ones that need either a fresh run or a correction to the prose. The per-file sections after that hold everything, including the WITNESSED and REPRODUCED rows, as the full record.

2026-09-10, later the same night: the action list below was worked, one file editor per file, and the outcomes were applied back into this ledger. Of the 162 rows the audit had routed to a file editor (94 STALE, 66 UNWITNESSED, and 2 NOT-A-MEASUREMENT rows a FRONTIER-2026.md editor found while working the same paragraph), 70 were CORRECTED to the number the current witness states, 9 were DATED (the original number kept as the record of that date, with a bracket giving the current witnessed value), 23 gained a witness through a cheap re-measurement this pass and are folded back into the WITNESSED count above, 18 were REWRITTEN either to state only what is witnessed or to keep a dated figure with a current-value bracket, and 42 were LEFT. LEFT does not mean skipped: every LEFT row was checked directly against its witness and against the file's own text, and each one turned out to already match (a table that counts out right, a summary line that reproduces exactly) or to have no prose sentence in the source file for the flagged number to attach to (a structural description of a table's shape, or a line-drift artifact in the audit's own line numbers), or, for a run of UNWITNESSED claims drawn from one paragraph, to be covered by a single unwitnessed-in-place note added on a sibling row from the same sentence. The note column on every touched row keeps the original finding and appends what was done about it, prefixed "Worked 2026-09-10".

## The action list, as worked

Every claim that was STALE or UNWITNESSED when the audit ran, grouped by source file in the same order as the sections below, shown here with the action a file editor took and its outcome. STALE meant the witness file existed but the number it showed no longer matched the claim; UNWITNESSED meant no witness could be found at all. The status column below is now the action taken (CORRECTED, DATED, WITNESSED, REWRITTEN, or LEFT), not the original finding; the note column keeps the original finding and appends what was done, prefixed "Worked 2026-09-10".

94 rows that were STALE and 66 that were UNWITNESSED, 160 total, across 16 files; every row below now carries the action taken and its outcome instead of its original status.
